\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{2}K\varepsilon\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big($}}}}}C_{d,K,T}(T^{3}+T)e^{C_{d,K,T}T}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big)$}}}}}^{1/2}.

∎

Lemma 5.4
.

Suppose that Assumption (A) holds and let
(t,\mu)\in[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
. If there exists
q>2
such that
\mu\in\mathcal{P}_{q}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
, then
\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\lim_{m\to\infty}v_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\mu)=v_{\varepsilon}(t,\mu)
.

Proof.

The proof goes as the same line as
[
17
, Theorem A.6]
with
[
23
, Theorem 3.1, Theorem 3.6]
replacing the limit theory in
[
17
]
, because of the appearance of the common noise.
∎

Lemma 5.5
.

Suppose that
Assumptions (A*) and (B)
hold. The function
\overline{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}:[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{dn}\to\mathbb{R}
defined by

\displaystyle\overline{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})=\overline{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,x^{1},\ldots,x^{n}):=\widetilde{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\delta_{x^{1}}\otimes\ldots\otimes\delta_{x^{n}})

(5.16)

is a classical solution of the Bellman equation

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}u(t,\overline{x})+\sup_{\overline{a}\in A^{n}}\Bigg\{\dfrac{1}{n}\sum^{n}_{i=1}f^{i}_{n,m}(t,\overline{x},a^{i})+\dfrac{1}{2}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\textup{tr}\left[\Big((\sigma\sigma^{\top})(t,x^{i},a^{i})+(\sigma^{0}\sigma^{0;\top})(t)+\varepsilon^{2}I_{d}\Big)\partial_{x^{i}x^{i}}^{2}u(t,\overline{x})\right]\\
&\hskip 80.0pt+\sum^{n}_{i=1}\left\langle b^{i}_{n,m}(t,\overline{x},a^{i}),\partial_{x^{i}}u(t,\overline{x})\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i,j=1,i\neq j}^{n}\textup{tr}\Big[(\sigma^{0}\sigma^{0;\top})(t)\partial_{x^{i}x^{j}}^{2}u(t,\overline{x})\Big]\Bigg\}\\
&=0\hskip 5.0pt\text{in $[0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{dn}$};\\
&u(T,\overline{x})=\dfrac{1}{n}\sum^{n}_{i=1}g^{i}_{n,m}(\overline{x})\hskip 5.0pt\text{ in $\mathbb{R}^{dn}$},\end{aligned}\right.

(5.17)

where
\overline{a}=(a^{1},\ldots,a^{n})
with
a^{i}\in A
for
i=1,2,\ldots,n
. Moreover, there are constants
C_{4}=C_{4}(K)
and
C_{n,m}
such that for any
\varepsilon>0
,
n,m\in\mathbb{N}
,
t,t^{\prime}\in[0,T]
and
\overline{x},\overline{x}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{dn}
, we have

\displaystyle|\partial_{x^{i}}\overline{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})|\leq\dfrac{C_{4}}{n}\hskip 5.0pt\text{for every $i=1,2,\ldots,n$ and}\hskip 10.0pt

(5.18)

\displaystyle-C_{n,m}\leq\partial^{2}_{\overline{x}_{j}\overline{x}_{k}}\overline{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})\leq\dfrac{C_{n,m}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\hskip 5.0pt\text{for every $j,k=1,2,\ldots,dn$,}

where we denote by
\partial_{\overline{x}_{j}\overline{x}_{k}}^{2}v(t,\overline{x})\in\mathbb{R}
the second order derivative with respect to
\overline{x}_{j}
and
\overline{x}_{k}
for
j,k=1,2,\ldots,dn
.

Proof.

We refer readers to
[
17
, Theorem A.7]
for proving that
\overline{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}
is a classical solution of the Bellman equation to (
5.17
). From
[
31
, Chapter 4.7, Theorem 4]
, we deduce the estimate of the second order derivative in (
5.18
). It remains to prove the estimate of the first order derivative in (
5.18
). We would like to point out that the proof presented below differs from that in
[
17
, Theorem A.7]
. Specifically, we were unable to reproduce identity
[
17
, (A.21)]
therein, which may be attributed to a possible typographical error. As
\overline{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})\in C^{1,2}([0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{dn})
, we notice that it is enough to establish that

\left|\overline{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})-\overline{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{z})\right|\leq\frac{C_{4}}{n}\left|\overline{x}-\overline{z}\right|,

when the components of
\overline{x}=(x^{1},\ldots,x^{n})
and
\overline{z}=(z^{1},\ldots,z^{n})
are all equal, apart from one component
x^{k}\neq z^{k}
for some
k=1,2,\ldots,n
. Recalling the relations in (
5.14
) and (
5.16
), we use the continuity in (4) of Lemma
5.2
to yield that

\displaystyle\left|\overline{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})-\overline{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{z})\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2K\sup_{\overline{\alpha}\in\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{n}_{t}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{t}^{T}\left|\overline{X}_{s}^{i,m,\varepsilon,t,\overline{x},\overline{\alpha}}-\overline{X}_{s}^{i,m,\varepsilon,t,\overline{z},\overline{\alpha}}\right|ds+\left|\overline{X}_{T}^{i,m,\varepsilon,t,\overline{x},\overline{\alpha}}-\overline{X}_{T}^{i,m,\varepsilon,t,\overline{z},\overline{\alpha}}\right|\right].

(5.19)

Suppose that
\overline{x}
and
\overline{z}
differ only for the first component
x^{1}\neq z^{1}
. Fix
i=1,2,\ldots,n
, the process
\overline{X}^{i}:=\overline{X}_{s}^{i,m,\varepsilon,t,\overline{x},\overline{\alpha}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
solves the following equation on
[t,T]
:

\overline{X}_{s}^{i}=x^{i}+\int_{t}^{s}b_{n,m}^{i}(r,\overline{X}_{r}^{1},\ldots,\overline{X}_{r}^{n},\overline{\alpha}_{r}^{i})dr+\int_{t}^{s}\sigma(r,\overline{X}_{r}^{i},\overline{\alpha}_{r}^{i})d\overline{W}^{i}_{r}+\int_{t}^{s}\sigma^{0}(r)d\overline{W}^{0}_{r}+\varepsilon(\overline{B}^{i}_{s}-\overline{B}^{i}_{t}).

As the coefficients of the above equation are smooth and having bounded derivatives by Lemma
5.2
and Assumption (B),
[
38
, Chapter V, Theorem 39]
allows us to differentiate the above process with respect to
x^{1}_{k}\in\mathbb{R}
for some fixed
k=1,2,\ldots,d
to obtain that

\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}=e_{k}\delta_{1i}+\sum_{j=1}^{n}\int_{t}^{s}\partial_{x^{j}}b_{n,m}^{i}(r,\overline{X}_{r}^{1},\ldots,\overline{X}_{r}^{n},\overline{\alpha}_{r}^{i})\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{j}dr+\int_{t}^{s}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big($}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{i}\cdot\partial_{x}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big)$}}}}}\sigma(r,\overline{X}_{r}^{i},\overline{\alpha}_{r}^{i})d\overline{W}^{i}_{r},

where
\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big($}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{i}\cdot\partial_{x}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big)$}}}}}\sigma(r,\overline{X}_{r}^{i},\overline{\alpha}_{r}^{i})=\sum_{l=1}^{d}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big($}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{i}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big)$}}}}}}_{l}\partial_{x_{l}}\sigma(r,\overline{X}_{r}^{i},\overline{\alpha}_{r}^{i})\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
and
e_{k}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
is the standard basis vector in
\mathbb{R}^{d}
such that
(e_{k})_{j}=\delta_{kj}
. The
\mathbb{R}^{dn}
-valued continuous process
\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}:=(\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}^{1},\ldots,\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}^{n})^{\top}
is the unique solution to the above system of linear stochastic equations such that
\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{s\in[t,T]}|\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}|^{2}\right]<\infty
. Next, we provide an estimate of
\max_{s\in[t,T]}\mathbb{E}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}^{i}_{s}|\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}
with a method akin to the proof of Tanaka’s formula. Letting
\vartheta\in\mathbb{R}
, we consider the function
u_{\vartheta}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}
defined by

\displaystyle u_{\vartheta}(y):=\sqrt{|y|^{2}+\vartheta^{2}}.

Direct calculation gives

\displaystyle\partial_{y}u_{\vartheta}(y)=\frac{y}{u_{\vartheta}(y)},\quad\partial_{yy}^{2}u_{\vartheta}(x)=\frac{1}{u_{\vartheta}(y)}I_{d}-\frac{1}{\big[u_{\vartheta}(y)\big]^{3}}yy^{\top}.

Applying Itô formula to
u_{\vartheta}\left(\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}^{i}_{s}\right)
gives

\displaystyle\hskip-10.0ptdu_{\vartheta}\left(\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}^{i}_{s}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{327680}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\big\langle$}}}}\partial_{y}u_{\vartheta}(\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}^{i}_{s}),d\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{327680}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{327680}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{327680}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{327680}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big\rangle$}}}}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\left\{\left[\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big($}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}\cdot\partial_{x}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big)$}}}}}\sigma(s,\overline{X}_{s}^{i},\overline{\alpha}_{s}^{i})\right]^{\top}\partial^{2}_{yy}u_{\vartheta}\left(\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}^{i}_{s}\right)\left[\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big($}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}\cdot\partial_{x}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big)$}}}}}\sigma(s,\overline{X}_{s}^{i},\overline{\alpha}_{s}^{i})\right]\right\}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\frac{\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}}{u_{\vartheta}(\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i})},\sum_{j=1}^{n}\partial_{x^{j}}b_{n,m}^{i}(s,\overline{X}_{s}^{1},\ldots,\overline{X}_{s}^{n},\overline{\alpha}_{s}^{i})\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{j}ds+\left[\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big($}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}\cdot\partial_{x}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big)$}}}}}\sigma(s,\overline{X}_{s}^{i},\overline{\alpha}_{s}^{i})\right]d\overline{W}^{i}_{s}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\left\{\left[\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big($}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}\cdot\partial_{x}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big)$}}}}}\sigma(s,\overline{X}_{s}^{i},\overline{\alpha}_{s}^{i})\right]^{\top}\left(\frac{1}{u_{\vartheta}(\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i})}I_{d}-\frac{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big($}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big)$}}}}}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432 It is clear that

\displaystyle\frac{\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}}{u_{\vartheta}(\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i})}\longrightarrow\frac{\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}}}\mathbbm{1}_{\big\{\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}\neq 0\big\}},\quad\mathbb{P}\text{-a.s. as $\vartheta\to 0$.}

Moreover,

\displaystyle\hskip-10.0pt\frac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\left\{\left[\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big($}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}\cdot\partial_{x}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big)$}}}}}\sigma(s,\overline{X}_{s}^{i},\overline{\alpha}_{s}^{i})\right]^{\top}\left(\frac{1}{u_{\vartheta}(\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i})}I_{d}\right)\left[\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big($}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}\cdot\partial_{x}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big)$}}}}}\sigma(s,\overline{X}_{s}^{i},\overline{\alpha}_{s}^{i})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{1}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}}}\mathbbm{1}_{\big\{\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}\neq The term in the third line of (

) can be estimated by Cauchy–Schwarz inequality

\displaystyle\hskip-10.0pt\frac{1}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{i}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}}}\textup{tr}\left\{\left[\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big($}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{i}\cdot\partial_{x}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big)$}}}}}\sigma(r,\overline{X}_{r}^{i},\overline{\alpha}_{r}^{i})\right]^{\top}\left[\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big($}}}}}{\raise \displaystyle=\,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{i}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}}}\sum_{p,q=1}^{d}\left|\sum_{l=1}^{d}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big($}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{i}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big)$}}}}}}_{l}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\bi 5.2
) can be estimated by

\displaystyle\hskip-10.0pt\frac{1}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{i}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}}^{3}}\textup{tr}\left\{\left[\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big($}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{i}\cdot\partial_{x}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big)$}}}}}\sigma(r,\overline{X}_{r}^{i},\overline{\alpha}_{r}^{i})\right]^{\top}\left(\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{i}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big($}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{i}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big)$}}}}}^{\top}\right)\left[\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big($}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{i}\cdot\partial_{x}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big)$}}}}}\sigma(r,\overline{X}_{r}^{i},\overline{\alpha}_{r}^{i})\right]\right\}\mathbbm{1}_{\big\{\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{i}\neq 0\big\}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{i}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}}^{3}}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{i}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}}^{4}\left|\partial_{x}\sigma(s,\overline{X}_{r}^{i},\overline{\alpha}_{r}^{i})\right|^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\big\{\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{i}\neq 0\big\}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle dK^{2}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{i}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}}.

Therefore, (
5.2
) reduces to

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}|\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}^{i}_{s}|\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{1i}+\mathbb{E}\left[\int^{s}_{t}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{x^{j}}b_{n,m}^{i}(r,\overline{X}_{r}^{1},\ldots,\overline{X}_{r}^{n},\overline{\alpha}_{r}^{i})\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{j}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}+dK^{2}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}}dr\right]

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{1i}+\mathbb{E}\left[\int^{s}_{t}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{x^{j}}b_{n,m}^{i}(r,\overline{X}_{r}^{1},\ldots,\overline{X}_{r}^{n},\overline{\alpha}_{r}^{i})\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{j}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}+dK^{2}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}}dr\right].

Summing over
i=1,2,\ldots,n
, we have

\displaystyle\hskip-10.0pt\mathbb{E}\left[\sum^{n}_{i=1}\big|\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}\big|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle 1+\int^{s}_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big|\partial_{x^{j}}b_{n,m}^{i}(r,\overline{X}_{r}^{1},\ldots,\overline{X}_{r}^{n},\overline{\alpha}_{r}^{i})\big|\right)\big|\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{j}\big|+dK^{2}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{i}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}}\right]dr

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle 1+\int^{s}_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{1\leq\ell\leq n}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big|\partial_{x^{\ell}}b_{n,m}^{i}(r,\overline{X}_{r}^{1},\ldots,\overline{X}_{r}^{n},\overline{\alpha}_{r}^{i})\big|\right)\sum^{n}_{j=1}\big|\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{j}\big|+dK^{2}\sum^{n}_{j=1}|\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}^{j}_{r}|\right]dr.

The Lipschitz continuity estimate for
b_{n,m}^{i}
in Lemma
5.2
deduces that

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq\ell\leq n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|\partial_{x^{\ell}}b_{n,m}^{i}(s,\overline{X}_{s}^{1},\ldots,\overline{X}_{s}^{n},\overline{\alpha}_{s}^{i})\right|

\displaystyle=\max_{1\leq\ell\leq n}\left(\left|\partial_{x^{\ell}}b_{n,m}^{\ell}(s,\overline{X}_{s}^{1},\ldots,\overline{X}_{s}^{n},\overline{\alpha}_{s}^{\ell})\right|+\sum_{i=1,i\neq\ell}^{n}\left|\partial_{x^{\ell}}b_{n,m}^{i}(s,\overline{X}_{s}^{1},\ldots,\overline{X}_{s}^{n},\overline{\alpha}_{s}^{i})\right|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{d}\left[K\left(1+\frac{1}{n}\right)+K\frac{n-1}{n}\right]

\displaystyle=2\sqrt{d}K.

It gives us that
\mathbb{E}\left[\sum^{n}_{i=1}\big|\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}\big|\right]\leq 1+(2\sqrt{d}K+dK^{2})\int_{t}^{s}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum^{n}_{i=1}\big|\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{r}^{i}\big|\right]\,dr
. Grönwall’s inequality yields

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum^{n}_{i=1}\big|\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}\big|\right]\leq e^{(2\sqrt{d}K+dK^{2})T},\quad\text{for every $s\in[t,T]$}.

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum^{n}_{i=1}\big|\partial_{x^{1}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}\big|\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum^{n}_{i=1}\left(\sum^{d}_{k=1}\big|\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}\big|^{2}\right)^{1/2}\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\sum^{n}_{i=1}\sum^{d}_{k=1}\big|\partial_{x^{1}_{k}}\overline{X}_{s}^{i}\big|\right]\leq de^{(2\sqrt{d}K+dK^{2})T},

(5.21)

for every
s\in[t,T]
.

Finally, recalling that
\overline{x}
and
\overline{z}
differ only for the first component
x^{1}\neq z^{1}
, we define

\displaystyle F_{s}^{i,x^{1}}:=\overline{X}_{s}^{i,m,\varepsilon,t,(x^{1},\ldots,x^{n}),\overline{\alpha}}.

We obtain from (
5.19
) and (
5.21
) that

\displaystyle\left|\overline{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})-\overline{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{z})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2K}{n}\sup_{\overline{\alpha}\in\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{n}_{t}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{t}^{T}\left|F_{s}^{i,x^{1}+(z^{1}-x^{1})}-F_{s}^{i,x^{1}}\right|ds+\left|F_{T}^{i,x^{1}+(z^{1}-x^{1})}-F_{T}^{i,x^{1}}\right|\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{2K}{n}\sup_{\overline{\alpha}\in\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{n}_{t}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{t}^{T}\left|\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{y^{1}}F^{i,y^{1}}_{s}(z^{1}-x^{1})d\lambda\right|ds+\left|\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{y^{1}}F^{i,y^{1}}_{T}(z^{1}-x^{1})d\lambda\right|\right]\Bigg|_{y^{1}=x^{1}+\lambda(z^{1}-x^{1})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2K}{n}\sup_{\overline{\alpha}\in\overline{\mathcal{A}}^{n}_{t}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{t}^{T}\int_{0}^{1}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{y^{1}}F^{i,y^{1}}_{s}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\Big|_{y^{1}=x^{1}+\lambda(z^{1}-x^{1})}d\lambda ds+\int_{0}^{1}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{y^{1}}F^{i,y^{1}}_{T}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\Big|_{y^{1}=x^{1}+\lambda(z^{1}-x^{1})}d\lambda\right]|z^{1}-x^{1}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{d,K,T}}{n}|z^{1}-x^{1}|.

∎

Theorem 5.6
.

Suppose that Assumptions (A)-(B) hold. For every
\varepsilon>0
,

n,m\in\mathbb{N}
, the function
\overline{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}
defined in (
5.16
) and the function
v_{\varepsilon,n,m}
defined in (
5.15
) satisfy the following:

1.

for any
(t,\mu)\in[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
, we have

\displaystyle v_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\mu)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\overline{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,x^{1},\ldots,x^{n})\mu(dx^{1})\otimes\ldots\otimes\mu(dx^{n})\,;

(5.22)

2.

v_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\mu)\in C_{1}^{1,2}([0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}))
;

3.

there is a constant
\ell_{2}=\ell_{2}(K,T)
such that for any
(t,\mu)\in[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
, we have
|v_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\mu)|\leq\ell_{2}
;

4.

v_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\mu)
solves the following equation in the classical sense

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}u(t,\mu)\\
&+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\sup_{\overline{a}\in A^{n}}\Bigg\{\dfrac{1}{n}\sum^{n}_{i=1}f^{i}_{n,m}(t,\overline{x},a^{i})+\dfrac{1}{2}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\textup{tr}\left[\Big((\sigma\sigma^{\top})(t,x^{i},a^{i})+(\sigma^{0}\sigma^{0;\top})(t)+\varepsilon^{2}I_{d}\Big)\partial_{x^{i}x^{i}}^{2}\overline{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})\right]\\
&\hskip 75.0pt+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i,j=1,i\neq j}^{n}\textup{tr}\Big[(\sigma^{0}\sigma^{0;\top})(t)\partial_{x^{i}x^{j}}^{2}\overline{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})\Big]\\
&\hskip 75.0pt+\sum^{n}_{i=1}\left\langle b^{i}_{n,m}(t,\overline{x},a^{i}),\partial_{x^{i}}\overline{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})\right\rangle\Bigg\}\mu(dx^{1})\otimes\ldots\otimes\mu(dx^{n})\\
&=0\hskip 5.0pt\text{for any $(t,\mu)\in[0,T)\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})$};\\
&u(T,\mu)=\dfrac{1}{n}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}g^{i}_{n,m}(\overline{x})\mu(dx^{1})\otimes\ldots\otimes\mu(dx^{n})\hskip 5.0pt\text{for any $\mu\in\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})$}.\end{aligned}\right.

Proof.

The proof follows from Lemma
5.5
and
[
17
, Theorem A.7]
.
∎

6
Viscosity Solution Theory

Our main goal in this paper is to show that the value function defined by (
3.6
) is the unique viscosity solution to the following HJB equation:

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}u(t,\mu)+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sup_{a\in A}\Bigg\{f(t,x,\mu,a)+b(t,x,\mu,a)\cdot\partial_{\mu}u(t,\mu)(x)\\
&+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big(\sigma(t,x,a)\big[\sigma(t,x,a)\big]^{\top}\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}u(t,\mu)(x)\Big)\Bigg\}\mu(dx)+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big[\sigma^{0}(t)[\sigma^{0}(t)]^{\top}\mathcal{H}u(t,\mu)\Big]\\
&=0\hskip 5.0pt\text{for $(t,\mu)\in[0,T)\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})$};\\
&u(T,\mu)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}g(x,\mu)\mu(dx)\hskip 10.0pt\text{for $\mu\in\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})$}.\end{aligned}\right.

(6.1)

We choose the set of test function to be
PC_{1}^{1,2}([0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}))
. Now, we are ready to define our notion of viscosity subsolution (resp. supersolution).

Definition 6.1
.

A continuous function
u:[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
is called a viscosity subsolution of equation (
6.1
) if

(1a).

u(T,\mu)\leq\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}g(x,\mu)\mu(dx)
, for every
\mu\in\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d});

(1b).

for any
(t,\mu)\in[0,T)\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
and
\varphi\in PC_{1}^{1,2}([0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}))
such that
u-\varphi
attains a maximum with a value of
0
at
(t,\mu)
, then the first equation of (
6.1
) holds with the inequality sign
\geq
replacing the equality sign and with
\varphi
replacing
u
.

For any function
h:\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})\to\mathbb{R}
, it could be naturally interpreted as a function with domain
\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A)
by
h(\nu):=h(\mu)
, with
\mu
being the marginal distribution of
\nu
on
\mathbb{R}^{d}
such that
\nu(\cdot\times A)=\mu(\cdot)
. We define the respective projections
\pi_{d}:\mathbb{R}^{2d}\to\mathbb{R}^{d}
and
\pi_{d\times d}:\mathbb{R}^{2d\times 2d}\to\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
such that for any
y=(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{2d})^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{2d}
and
M\in\mathbb{R}^{2d\times 2d}
, it holds that
\pi_{d}(y)=(y_{1},y_{2},\ldots,y_{d})^{\top}
and
(\pi_{d\times d}(M))_{ij}=M_{ij}
. A continuous function
u:[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
is called a viscosity supersolution of equation (
6.1
) if

(2a).

u(T,\mu)\geq\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}g(x,\mu)\mu(dx)
, for every
\mu\in\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d});

(2b).

for any
s_{0}\in[0,T]
,
(t,\nu)\in[0,s_{0})\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A)
and
\varphi\in PC_{1}^{1,2}([0,s_{0}]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A))
such that
u-\varphi
attains a minimum with a value of
0
at
(t,\nu)
over
[0,s_{0}]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A)
, the following inequality holds:

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\varphi(t,\nu)+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A}\Bigg\{f(t,x,\mu,a)+b(t,x,\mu,a)\cdot\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t,\nu)(x,a)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big((\sigma(t,x,a)\big[\sigma(t,x,a)\big]^{\top})\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t,\nu)(x,a)\Big)\Bigg\}\nu(dx,da)+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big[\sigma^{0}(t)[\sigma^{0}(t)]^{\top}\mathcal{H}_{d\times d}\varphi(t,\nu)\Big]

\displaystyle\leq 0,

(6.2)

where
\mu
is the marginal distribution of
\nu
on
\mathbb{R}^{d}
,
\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t,\nu)(\cdot,\cdot):=\pi_{d}(\partial_{\nu}\varphi(t,\nu)(\cdot,\cdot))
,
\mathcal{H}_{d\times d}\varphi:=\pi_{d\times d}(\mathcal{H}\varphi)
, recalling that
\partial_{\nu}\varphi(t,\nu)(\cdot,\cdot):\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A\to\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A
and
\mathcal{H}
maps functions on
\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A)
to
\mathbb{R}^{2d\times 2d}
.

A continuous function
u:[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
is called a viscosity solution of (
6.1
) if it is both a viscosity subsolution and a viscosity supersolution.

Remark 6.1
.

We note that this definition of viscosity supersolution is stronger than Crandall-Lions’ definition which is adopted by
[
17
]
. In fact, items (2a)-(2b) in Definition
6.1
are sufficient conditions for the usual Crandall-Lions’ definition of supersolution. Assume
u
is a viscosity supersolution satisfying items (2a)-(2b) in Definition
6.1
. Let
\varphi\in PC_{1}^{1,2}([0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}))
such that
u-\varphi
attains a minimum with a value of
0
at
(t_{0},\mu_{0})
. Now, we interpret both
u
,
\varphi
as functions on
[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A)
. Let
\xi\in L^{2}(\Omega,\mathcal{F},\mathbb{P};\mathbb{R}^{d})
,
\mathcal{L}(\xi)=\mu_{0}
, and
\alpha^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{t_{0}}
be arbitrary, where
\mathcal{M}_{t}
is the set of
\mathcal{F}_{t}^{t}
-measurable random variables. Then for
\nu_{0}:=\mathcal{L}(\xi,\alpha^{\prime})\in\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A)
,
u-\varphi
attains a minimum at
(t_{0},\nu_{0})
, therefore from (
(2b).
)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})+\mathbb{E}\bigg\{f(t_{0},\xi,\mu_{0},\alpha^{\prime})+\big[\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})(\xi)\cdot b(t_{0},\xi,\mu_{0},\alpha^{\prime})\big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\Big[\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})(\xi)\sigma(t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\prime})[\sigma(t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\prime})]^{\top}\Big]+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big[\sigma^{0}(t_{0})[\sigma^{0}(t_{0})]^{\top}\mathcal{H}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})\Big]\bigg\}\leq 0.

Since
\alpha^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{t_{0}}
is arbitrary, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})+\sup_{\alpha^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{t_{0}}}\mathbb{E}\bigg\{f(t_{0},\xi,\mu_{0},\alpha^{\prime})+\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})(\xi)\cdot b(t_{0},\xi,\mu_{0},\alpha^{\prime})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\Big[\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})(\xi)\sigma(t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\prime})[\sigma(t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\prime})]^{\top}\Big]+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big[\sigma^{0}(t_{0})[\sigma^{0}(t_{0})]^{\top}\mathcal{H}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})\Big]\bigg\}\leq 0.

We claim that the above is equivalent to the following:

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sup_{a\in A}\Bigg\{f(t_{0},x,\mu_{0},a)+b(t_{0},x,\mu_{0},a)\cdot\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})(x)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big[\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})(x)\sigma(t_{0},x,a)[\sigma(t_{0},x,a)]^{\top}\Big]\Bigg\}\mu_{0}(dx)+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big[\sigma^{0}(t_{0})[\sigma^{0}(t_{0})]^{\top}\mathcal{H}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})\Big]\leq 0.

(6.3)

Define
H(t,x,\mu,a):=f(t,x,\mu,a)+\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t,\mu)(x)\cdot b(t,x,\mu,a)+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\big[\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t,\mu)(x)\sigma(t,x,a)\sigma(t,x,a)^{\top}\big]
, we aim to show that

\displaystyle\sup_{\alpha^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{t_{0}}}\mathbb{E}H(t_{0},\xi,\mu_{0},\alpha^{\prime})=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sup_{a\in A}H(t_{0},x,\mu_{0},a)\mu_{0}(dx),\quad\text{ for $\xi\in L^{2}(\Omega,\mathcal{F},\mathbb{P};\mathbb{R}^{d})$ such that $\mathcal{L}(\xi)=\mu_{0}$}.

To see this, we observe that for any
\alpha^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{t_{0}}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}H(t_{0},\xi,\mu_{0},\alpha^{\prime})\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sup_{a\in A}H(t_{0},x,\mu_{0},a)\mu_{0}(dx),

and thus
\sup_{\alpha^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{t_{0}}}\mathbb{E}H(t_{0},\xi,\mu_{0},\alpha^{\prime})\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sup_{a\in A}H(t_{0},x,\mu_{0},a)\mu_{0}(dx)
. Invoking the regularity of
f
,
\varphi
,
b
and
\sigma
, for any
\varepsilon>0
, we apply the measurable selection theorem (see for instance
[
43
]
) to find a
\alpha^{\varepsilon}\in\mathcal{M}_{t_{0}}
such that

\displaystyle\sup_{\alpha^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{t_{0}}}\mathbb{E}H(t_{0},\xi,\mu_{0},\alpha^{\prime})\geq\mathbb{E}H(t_{0},\xi,\mu_{0},\alpha^{\varepsilon})\geq\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sup_{a\in A}H(t_{0},x,\mu_{0},a)\mu_{0}(dx)-\varepsilon.

Since
\varepsilon
is arbitrary, we conclude the claim in (
6.3
), which is the classical Crandall-Lions’ definition.

Remark 6.2
.

We impose this stronger definition because when proving the comparison theorem for the viscosity supersolution, we encounter technical difficulties that the Crandall-Lions’ definition alone cannot resolve.
We believe that a similar issue arises in Step II of the proof of
[
17
, Theorem 5.1]
, resulting in certain gaps in the arguments presented therein.
Specifically, when we imitate the method used for the subsolution in the proof of Theorem
6.3
, it requires the inequality (
6.19
) but with a reversed sign. Due to the supremum taken and the integration in (
6.19
), only one direction of the inequality is allowed. To bypass this issue, we prove the comparison theorem by showing that for every step control, i.e., controls of the form
\alpha=\sum_{i=0}^{n-1}\mathfrak{a}_{i}1_{[t_{i},t_{i+1})}
, with
0=:t_{0}<t_{1}<\ldots<t_{n}:=T
and
\mathfrak{a}_{i}\in\mathcal{M}_{t_{i}}
(where
\mathcal{M}_{t}
is the set of
\mathcal{F}_{t}^{t}
-measurable random variables), the supersolution
u_{2}
satisfies

\displaystyle u_{2}(t,\mu)\geq J(t,\xi,\alpha),

(6.4)

for every
(t,\xi)\in[0,T]\times L^{2}(\Omega,\mathcal{F},\mathbb{P};\mathbb{R}^{d})
such that
\mu=\mathcal{L}(\xi)
. Clearly this leads to
u_{2}\geq v
where
v
is the value function defined in (
3.6
). By using inductive arguments, it is sufficient to prove (
6.4
) by proving that for every
\underline{t},\overline{t}\in[0,T]
with
\underline{t}<\overline{t}
, it holds that

\displaystyle u_{2}(\underline{t},\mu)\geq\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\int_{\underline{t}}^{\overline{t}}f\left(r,X_{r}^{\underline{t},\xi,\mathfrak{a}},\mathbb{P}_{X_{r}^{\underline{t},\xi,\mathfrak{a}}},\mathfrak{a}\right)dr+u_{2}(\overline{t},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X^{\underline{t},\xi,\mathfrak{a}}_{\overline{t}}})\Bigg],

for every
\xi\in L^{2}((\Omega,\mathcal{F},\mathbb{P});\mathbb{R}^{d})
with
\mathcal{L}(\xi)=\mu
, and
\mathfrak{a}\in\mathcal{M}_{\underline{t}}
. It is not hard to see that the right-hand side is law invariant with respect to the joint law of the initial random variable and the control. Thus,
v^{*}(t,\nu):=\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{t}^{\overline{t}}f\left(r,X_{r}^{t,\xi,\mathfrak{a}},\mathbb{P}_{X_{r}^{t,\xi,\mathfrak{a}}},\mathfrak{a}\right)dr+u_{2}(\overline{t},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X^{t,\xi,\mathfrak{a}}_{\overline{t}}})\right]
is a real-valued function on
[0,\overline{t}]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A)
with
\nu
being the joint law of the initial random variable and the control. It seems that we must involve the function
v^{*}_{n,m}(t,\nu)
, the smooth and finite-dimensional approximation of
v^{*}(t,\nu)
, as a part of the test function used for comparison. The Crandall-Lions’ definition cannot handle cases where the minimum point lies in
[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A)
. A natural approach is to modify the test function on
[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A)
to be a function on
[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
. However, finding such a modification that remains within our set of test functions and retains the necessary information (such as the
L
-derivatives) is challenging. For instance, we are unable to treat
v^{*}_{n,m}(t,\nu)
as a function on
[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
for a fixed control
\mathfrak{a}
directly, as
v^{*}_{n,m}(t,\nu)
is not law invariant with respect to the marginal of
\nu
on
\mathbb{R}^{d}
(law of
\xi
). Furthermore, we may define
\underline{v}^{*}:[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})\to\mathbb{R}
by

\displaystyle\underline{v}^{*}(t,\mu)=\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\text{$\nu\in\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A)$ s.t.}\\
\text{marginal of $\nu$ on $\mathbb{R}^{d}$ is $\mu$}\end{subarray}}v_{n,m}^{*}(t,\nu),

However, there is no guarantee that
\underline{v}^{*}(t,\mu)
is
L
-differentiable with respect to
\mu
. Moreover, even if it is
L
-differentiable, it is not certain that it could be related to
\partial_{\nu}v_{n,m}^{*}
. This issue seems to also arise in Step II of the proof of
[
17
, Theorem 5.1]
. Finally, we remark that assuming non-degenerate volatility does not remedy this issue.

6.1
Existence

Theorem 6.2
.

Suppose that Assumption (A) holds. The value function
v
defined in (
3.6
) is a viscosity solution of (
6.1
).

Proof.

Part 1.
v
is a viscosity supersolution:
For any
s_{0}^{\prime}\in[0,T]
and
\varphi\in PC_{1}^{1,2}([0,s_{0}^{\prime}]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A))
, we assume that
v-\varphi
attains a minimum at
(t_{0},\nu_{0})

\in[0,s_{0}^{\prime}]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A))
with a value of
0
. Recalling that
\mathcal{M}_{t}
is the set of
\mathcal{F}_{t}^{t}
-measurable random variables, let
\alpha^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{t_{0}}
and
\xi\in L^{2}((\Omega^{1},\mathcal{F}_{t}^{1},\mathbb{P}^{1});\mathbb{R}^{d})
such that
\mathcal{L}(\xi,\alpha^{\prime})=\nu_{0}
. Letting
a\in A
be arbitrary, we define
\alpha_{s}:=a\mathbbm{1}_{[0,t_{0})}(s)+\alpha^{\prime}\mathbbm{1}_{[t_{0},T]}(s)
, which belongs to
\mathcal{A}_{t_{0}}
. Let
(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha})_{s\in[t_{0},T]}
be the solution of the dynamic (
2.3
) with initial time
t_{0}
, initial data
\xi
and control
\alpha
defined in the above. Then for
h>0
small enough, we use the dynamic programming in Theorem
3.5
and the Itô formula in Theorem
2.6
to obtain that

\displaystyle 0\geq\,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{h}\mathbb{E}\Big[(v-\varphi)(t_{0},\nu_{0})-(v-\varphi)(t_{0}+h,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{(X_{t_{0}+h}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{t_{0}+h})})\Big]

\displaystyle\geq\,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{h}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{0}+h}f(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha}},\alpha_{s})+\partial_{t}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}_{(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{s})}^{W^{0}})

\displaystyle\hskip 30.0pt+\mathbb{E}^{1}\big[\partial_{\mu}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{s})})(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{s})\cdot b(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha}},\alpha_{s})\big]

\displaystyle\hskip 30.0pt+\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}^{1}\Big\{\operatorname{tr}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{s})})(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{s})\sigma(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{s})\sigma^{\top}(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{s})\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\Big\}

\displaystyle\hskip 30.0pt+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\mathcal{H}_{d\times d}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{s})})\sigma^{0}(s)[\sigma^{0}(s)]^{\top}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}ds\Bigg].

We proceed as follows: for any
\varepsilon>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}^{0}\left(\sup_{t\in[s,s+h]}\mathcal{W}_{2}(\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{t}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha}},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha}})>\varepsilon\right)\leq\,

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbb{E}^{0}\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[s,s+h]}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\mathcal{W}_{2}(\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{t}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha}},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha}})\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}}^{2}}{\varepsilon^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbb{E}\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[s,s+h]}|X_{t}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha}-X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha}|^{2}}{\varepsilon^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle\frac{Ch}{\varepsilon^{2}},

where in the last inequality we have used Proposition
3.1
. The above term goes to
0
as
h\to 0
, thus the flow of measure
s\mapsto\mathbb{P}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha}}^{W^{0}}
is also continuous for
\mathbb{P}
-a.s.
\omega^{0}\in\Omega^{0}
and for any
s\in[t_{0},T]
, so is
s\mapsto\mathbb{P}_{(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{s})}^{W^{0}}
for
\mathbb{P}
-a.s.
\omega^{0}\in\Omega^{0}
and any
s\in[t_{0},T]
. From the regularity of the coefficients in Assumption (A), the regularity of the test function, the continuity of the process
s\mapsto X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha}
for
\mathbb{P}
-a.s.
\omega\in\Omega
and the dominated convergence theorem, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{1}{h}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{0}+h}\Big[f(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha}},\alpha_{s})+\partial_{t}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}_{(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{s})}^{W^{0}})+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\mathcal{H}_{d\times d}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{s})})\sigma^{0}(s)[\sigma^{0}(s)]^{\top}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\Big]ds\right\}

\displaystyle\longrightarrow\mathbb{E}\bigg\{f(t_{0},\xi,\mu_{0},\alpha^{\prime})+\partial_{t}\varphi(t_{0},\nu_{0})+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\mathcal{H}_{d\times d}\varphi(t_{0},\nu_{0})\sigma^{0}(s)[\sigma^{0}(s)]^{\top}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\bigg\}\quad\text{ as }h\to 0.

(6.5)

We claim that the map
[t_{0},t_{0}+h]\ni s\mapsto\mathbb{E}\mathbb{E}^{1}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\partial_{\mu}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{s})})(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{s})\cdot b(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha}},\alpha_{s})\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}
is continuous. Writing
\partial_{\mu}\varphi_{s}\cdot b_{s}:=\partial_{\mu}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{s})})(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{s})\cdot b(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha}},\alpha_{s})
for ease of notation, the continuity of
\partial_{\mu}\varphi
,
b
,
X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha}
and
\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{s})}
imply the continuity of
\partial_{\mu}\varphi_{s}\cdot b_{s}
with respect to
s
for
\mathbb{P}
-a.s.
\omega\in\Omega
. Furthermore, due to Markov’s inequality, Definition
2.4
and Proposition
3.1
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in[t_{0},t_{0}+h]}\mathbb{E}\mathbb{E}^{1}\Big[|\partial_{\mu}\varphi_{s}\cdot b_{s}|\mathbbm{1}_{\{|\partial_{\mu}\varphi_{s}\cdot b_{s}|\geq M\}}\Big]\leq\,

\displaystyle K\sup_{s\in[t_{0},t_{0}+h]}\mathbb{E}\mathbb{E}^{1}\Big[|\partial_{\mu}\varphi_{s}|\mathbbm{1}_{\{|\partial_{\mu}\varphi_{s}\cdot b_{s}|\geq M\}}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle K\sup_{s\in[t_{0},t_{0}+h]}\mathbb{E}\mathbb{E}^{1}\Big[|\partial_{\mu}\varphi_{s}|\mathbbm{1}_{\{K|\partial_{\mu}\varphi_{s}|\geq M\}}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle K\sup_{s\in[t_{0},t_{0}+h]}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\mathbb{E}^{1}|\partial_{\mu}\varphi_{s}|^{2}}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\mathbb{E}^{1}\mathbbm{1}_{\{K|\partial_{\mu}\varphi_{s}|\geq M\}}}

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle\frac{K^{2}}{M}\sup_{s\in[t_{0},t_{0}+h]}\mathbb{E}\mathbb{E}^{1}|\partial_{\mu}\varphi_{s}|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle\frac{K^{2}}{M}C_{\varphi}(1+\mathbb{E}|\xi|^{2}+\mathbb{E}|\alpha^{\prime}|^{2})\to 0\text{ as }M\to\infty.

(6.6)

The continuity of the concerned map thus follows from Vitali convergence theorem. The continuity of the map
s\mapsto\mathbb{E}\mathbb{E}^{1}\operatorname{tr}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{s})})(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{s})\sigma(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{s})\sigma^{\top}(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha},\alpha_{s})\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}
follows similarly. Hence, when
h\to 0
, together with (
6.1
) we conclude that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\varphi(t_{0},\nu_{0})+\mathbb{E}\bigg\{f(t_{0},\xi,\mu_{0},\alpha^{\prime})+\big[\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\nu_{0})(\xi,\alpha^{\prime})\cdot b(t_{0},\xi,\mu_{0},\alpha^{\prime})\big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\Big[\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\nu_{0})(\xi,\alpha^{\prime})\sigma(t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\prime})[\sigma(t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\prime})]^{\top}\Big]+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\mathcal{H}_{d\times d}\varphi(t_{0},\nu_{0})\sigma^{0}(t_{0})[\sigma^{0}(t_{0})]^{\top}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\bigg\}\leq 0.

Part 2.
v
is a viscosity subsolution:
For any
\varphi\in{PC}_{1}^{1,2}([0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}))
, we assume that
v-\varphi
attains a maximum at
(t_{0},\mu_{0})
with value
0
. Let
\varepsilon>0
and
\xi\in L^{2}(\Omega^{1},\mathcal{F}^{1},\mathbb{P}^{1};\mathbb{R}^{d})
satisfy
\mathcal{L}(\xi)=\mu_{0}
. There is an
\alpha^{\varepsilon}\in\mathcal{A}_{t}
such that

\displaystyle v(t_{0},\mu_{0})-\varepsilon<\,

\displaystyle\inf_{h\in[0,T-t_{0}]}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{0}+h}f(r,X_{r}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{r}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}},\alpha_{r}^{\varepsilon})dr+v(t_{0}+h,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}_{t_{0}+h}})\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{0}+h}f(r,X_{r}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{r}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}},\alpha_{r}^{\varepsilon})dr+v(t_{0}+h,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}_{t_{0}+h}})\Bigg],\,\forall\,h\in[0,T-t_{0}],

where
(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}})
solves the dynamic (
2.3
) with initial time
t_{0}
, initial data
\xi
and control
\alpha^{\varepsilon}
. Then Theorem
2.6
implies

\displaystyle 0\leq\,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{h}\mathbb{E}\Big[(v-\varphi)(t_{0},\mu_{0})-(v-\varphi)(t_{0}+h,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{t_{0}+h}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}})\Big]

\displaystyle<\,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{h}\mathbb{E}\Bigg\{\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{0}+h}f(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}},\alpha_{s}^{\varepsilon})+\partial_{t}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}}^{W^{0}})

\displaystyle\hskip 30.0pt+\mathbb{E}^{1}\Big[\partial_{\mu}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}})(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}})\cdot b(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}},\alpha_{s}^{\varepsilon})\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 30.0pt+\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}^{1}\Big\{\operatorname{tr}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}})(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}})\sigma(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}},\alpha_{s}^{\varepsilon})\sigma^{\top}(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}},\alpha_{s}^{\varepsilon})\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\Big\}

\displaystyle\hskip 30.0pt+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\mathcal{H}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}})\sigma^{0}(s)\sigma^{0;\top}(s)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}ds\Bigg\}+\varepsilon

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{h}\mathbb{E}\Bigg\{\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{0}+h}\sup_{a\in A}\bigg(f(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}},a)+\partial_{t}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}}^{W^{0}})

\displaystyle\hskip 80.0pt+\mathbb{E}^{1}\big[\partial_{\mu}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}})(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}})\cdot b(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}},a)\big]

\displaystyle\hskip 80.0pt+\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}^{1}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{327680}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\big\{$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{327680}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\big\{$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{327680}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\big\{$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{327680}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big\{$}}}}}\operatorname{tr}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}})(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}})\sigma(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}},a)\sigma^{\top}(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}},a)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\Big\}

\displaystyle\hskip 80.0pt+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\mathcal{H}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}})\sigma^{0}(s)\sigma^{0;\top}(s)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\bigg)ds\Bigg\}+\varepsilon.

Arguing as in (

), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{h}\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{0}+h}\partial_{t}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}}^{W^{0}})+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\Big[\mathcal{H}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}}^{W^{0}})\sigma^{0}(s)\sigma^{0;\top}(s)\Big]ds\right\}

\displaystyle\longrightarrow\partial_{t}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\mathcal{H}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})\sigma^{0}(t_{0})\sigma^{0;\top}(t_{0})\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\text{ as }h\to 0.

We estimate the following terms:

\displaystyle\textup{I}:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{h}\mathbb{E}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{0}+h}\sup_{a\in A}\Big\{f(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}},a)-f(t_{0},\xi,\mu_{0},a)\Big\}ds;

\displaystyle\textup{II}:=

\displaystyle\hskip 85.0pt-\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})(\xi)\cdot b(t_{0},\xi,\mu_{0},a)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\Big\}ds;
