\displaystyle\hskip 85.0pt-\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}^{1}\Big\{\operatorname{tr}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})(\xi)\sigma(t_{0},\xi,a)\sigma^{\top}(t_{0},\xi,a)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\Big\}\bigg\}ds.

Then the above gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{h}\mathbb{E}\Bigg\{\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{0}+h}\sup_{a\in A}\bigg(f(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}},a)+\mathbb{E}^{1}\Big[\partial_{\mu}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}})(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}})\cdot b(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}},a)\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 80.0pt+\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}^{1}\Big\{\operatorname{tr}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}})(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}})\sigma(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}},a)\sigma^{\top}(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}},a)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\Big\}\bigg)ds\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\textup{I}+\textup{II}+\textup{III}+\mathbb{E}\sup_{a\in A}\Big\{f(t_{0},\xi,\mu_{0},a)+\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})(\xi)\cdot b(t_{0},\xi,\mu_{0},a)

\displaystyle\hskip 110.0pt+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})(\xi)\sigma(t_{0},\xi,a)\sigma^{\top}(t_{0},\xi,a)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\Big\}.

We now show that all these three terms
I
,
II
,
\textup{III}\to 0
as

. We first investigate

\displaystyle\textup{I}=\,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{h}\mathbb{E}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{0}+h}\sup_{a\in A}\Big\{f(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}},a)-f(t_{0},\xi,\mu_{0},a)\Big\}ds

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{h}\mathbb{E}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{0}+h}K\Big\{|X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}-\xi|+\mathcal{W}_{2}(\mathbb{P}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}}^{W^{0}},\mu_{0})+|s-t_{0}|^{\beta}\Big\}ds

\displaystyle\hskip-10.0pt\longrightarrow 0,

as

by Proposition

, we have

\displaystyle\textup{II}=\,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{h}\mathbb{E}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{0}+h}\sup_{a\in A}\Big\{\mathbb{E}^{1}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\partial_{\mu}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}})(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}})\cdot b(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}},a)

\displaystyle\hskip 70.0pt-\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})(\xi)\cdot b(t_{0},\xi,\mu_{0},a)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\Big\}ds

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{h}\mathbb{E}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{0}+h}\sup_{a\in A}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}^{1}\Big[\partial_{\mu}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}})(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}})\cdot b(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}},a)\Big]

\displaystyle\,\hskip 70.0pt-\mathbb{E}^{1}\Big[\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})(\xi)\cdot b(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}},a)\Big]\bigg\}ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{h}\mathbb{E}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{0}+h}\sup_{a\in A}\mathbb{E}^{1}\Big[\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})(\xi)\cdot b(s,X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}},\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}},a)-\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})(\xi)\cdot b(t_{0},\xi,\mu_{0},a)\Big]ds

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle\frac{K}{h}\mathbb{E}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{0}+h}\mathbb{E}^{1}\Big[\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{\mu}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}})(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}})-\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})(\xi)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\Big]ds

\displaystyle+\frac{K}{h}\mathbb{E}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{0}+h}\mathbb{E}^{1}\Big[\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})(\xi)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big($}}}}}|s-t_{0}|^{\beta}+|X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}-\xi|+\mathcal{W}_{2}(\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}},\mu_{0})\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{327680}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big)$}}}}}\Big]ds

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle\frac{K}{h}\mathbb{E}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{0}+h}\mathbb{E}^{1}\Big[\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{\mu}\varphi(s,\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}})(X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}})-\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})(\xi)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\Big]ds

(6.7)

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{3}K}{h}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\mathbb{E}^{1}|\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})(\xi)|^{2}}\int_{t_{0}}^{t_{0}+h}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\mathbb{E}^{1}\Big(|s-t_{0}|^{2\beta}+|X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}-\xi|^{2}+\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\mathcal{W}_{2}(\mathbb{P}^{W^{0}}_{X_{s}^{t_{0},\xi,\alpha^{\varepsilon}}},\mu_{0})\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}}^{2}\Big)}ds.

The convergence of the term in (
6.7
) is due to the continuity as proved in (
6.6
). Thus, the term II converges to zero as
h\to 0
with the aid of Proposition
3.1
. Similar estimate holds for the term
III
, thus as
h\to 0
,

\displaystyle 0\leq\,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})+\mathbb{E}\sup_{a\in A}\bigg\{f(t_{0},\xi,\mu_{0},a)+\big[\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})(\xi)\cdot b(t_{0},\xi,a,\mu_{0})\big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})(\xi)\sigma(t_{0},\xi,a)\sigma^{\top}(t_{0},\xi,a)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\bigg\}+\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{tr}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\mathcal{H}\varphi(t_{0},\mu_{0})\sigma^{0}(t_{0})\sigma^{0;\top}(t_{0})\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}+\varepsilon.

∎

6.2
Comparison Theorem and Uniqueness

Theorem 6.3
.

Suppose that
Assumptions (A*) and (B)
hold. Let
u_{1}
,
u_{2}:[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})\to\mathbb{R}
be bounded functions such that they are the viscosity subsolution and supersolution (in the sense of Definition
6.1
) of equation (
6.1
) respectively. Then it holds that
u_{1}\leq u_{2}
on
[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
. Hence, the viscosity solution of equation (
6.1
) is unique.

Proof.

The following proof is inspired by
[
17
, Theorem 5.1]
. Recalling the function defined in (
5.3
) with
\varepsilon=0
, we shall prove that
u_{1}\leq v_{0}
and
v_{0}\leq u_{2}
on
[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
.

Part 1. Proof of
u_{1}\leq v_{0}
:

We prove by contradiction and suppose that there exists
(t_{0},\widetilde{\mu}_{0})\in[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
such that

\displaystyle(u_{1}-v_{0})(t_{0},\widetilde{\mu}_{0})>0.

Let
\xi\in L^{2}(\Omega,\mathcal{F},\mathbb{P};\mathbb{R}^{d})
such that
\mathcal{L}(\xi)=\widetilde{\mu}_{0}
. For any
k\in\mathbb{N}
, we let
\mu_{0}^{k}\in\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
be the law of
\xi\mathbbm{1}_{\{|\xi|\leq k\}}
. We see that
\mu_{0}^{k}\in\mathcal{P}_{q}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
for any
q\geq 1
and

\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\mathcal{W}_{2}(\mu_{0}^{k},\widetilde{\mu}_{0})\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}}^{2}\leq\mathbb{E}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}|\xi\mathbbm{1}_{\{|\xi|\leq k\}}-\xi|^{2}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}=\int_{|x|>k}|x|^{2}\widetilde{\mu}_{0}(dx)\longrightarrow 0

as
k\to\infty
. Therefore, as both
u_{1}
and
v_{0}
are continuous on
[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
, we can find a
k\in\mathbb{N}
large enough such that
\mu_{0}:=\mu_{0}^{k}\in\mathcal{P}_{q}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
for any
q\geq 1
and

\displaystyle(u_{1}-v_{0})(t_{0},\mu_{0})>0.

(6.8)

The purpose of adopting this
\mu_{0}:=\mu_{0}^{k}
instead of just working with
\widetilde{\mu}_{0}
is to apply the approximation theorem in Lemma
5.4
which requires the point
(t,\mu)
with
\mu
having a higher moment
q>2
.

Step 1A: Choice of the comparison function:

For any
\varepsilon>0
,
n,m\in\mathbb{N}
, we define
\widecheck{u}_{1}(t,\mu):=e^{t-t_{0}}u_{1}(t,\mu)
for any
(t,\mu)\in[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
. Recalling the definition in (
5.15
), (
5.6
), (
5.7
), we define similarly for
\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}
,
\widecheck{f}^{i}_{n,m}
,
\widecheck{f}
from
v_{\varepsilon,n,m}
,
f^{i}_{n,m}
,
f
respectively; we also define
\widecheck{g}:=e^{T-t_{0}}g
and
\widecheck{g}^{i}_{n,m}:=e^{T-t_{0}}g^{i}_{n,m}
. By direct computation, we see that
\widecheck{u}_{1}
is a viscosity subsolution of the equation

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}u(t,\mu)+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sup_{a\in A}\Bigg\{\widecheck{f}(t,x,\mu,a)+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big\{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}(\sigma\sigma^{\top})(t,x,a)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}u(t,\mu)(x)\Big\}\\
&\hskip 90.0pt+\Big\langle b(t,x,\mu,a),\partial_{\mu}u(t,\mu)(x)\Big\rangle\Bigg\}\mu(dx)+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big\{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}(\sigma^{0}\sigma^{0;\top})(t)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\mathcal{H}u(t,\mu)\Big\}-u(t,\mu)\\
&=0\hskip 5.0pt\text{for any $(t,\mu)\in[0,T)\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})$};\\
&u(T,\mu)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\widecheck{g}(x,\mu)\mu(dx)\hskip 5.0pt\text{for any $\mu\in\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})$}.\end{aligned}\right.

(6.9)

Besides, recalling the definition in (
5.16
), we define
\widecheck{\overline{v}}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x}):=e^{t-t_{0}}\overline{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})
where
\overline{x}=(x^{1},\ldots,x^{n})
for each
x^{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
with
i=1,2,\ldots,n
. By Theorem
5.6
, we obtain that
\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}
solves the following equation in the classical sense:

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}u(t,\mu)\\
&+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\sup_{\overline{a}\in A^{n}}\Bigg\{\dfrac{1}{n}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\widecheck{f}^{i}_{n,m}(t,\overline{x},a^{i})+\dfrac{1}{2}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\textup{tr}\left[\Big((\sigma\sigma^{\top})(t,x^{i},a^{i})+(\sigma^{0}\sigma^{0;\top})(t)+\varepsilon^{2}I_{d}\Big)\partial_{x^{i}x^{i}}^{2}\widecheck{\overline{v}}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})\right]\\
&\hskip 65.0pt+\dfrac{1}{2}\sum^{n}_{i,j=1,i\neq j}\textup{tr}\left[(\sigma^{0}\sigma^{0;\top})(t)\partial_{x^{i}x^{j}}^{2}\widecheck{\overline{v}}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})\right]\\
&\hskip 65.0pt+\sum^{n}_{i=1}\Big\langle b^{i}_{n,m}(t,\overline{x},a^{i}),\partial_{x^{i}}\widecheck{\overline{v}}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})\Big\rangle\Bigg\}\bbotimes_{k=1}^{n}\mu(dx^{k})-u(t,\mu)\\
&=0\hskip 5.0pt\text{for any $(t,\mu)\in[0,T)\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})$};\\
&u(T,\mu)=\dfrac{1}{n}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\widecheck{g}^{i}_{n,m}(\overline{x})\bbotimes_{k=1}^{n}\mu(dx^{k})\hskip 5.0pt\text{for any $\mu\in\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})$},\end{aligned}\right.

(6.10)

where we write
\displaystyle\bbotimes_{k=1}^{n}\mu(dx^{k}):=\mu(dx^{1})\otimes\ldots\otimes\mu(dx^{n})
and
\overline{a}=(a^{1},\ldots,a^{n})
for each
a^{i}\in A
with
i=1,2,\ldots,n
. As
v_{\varepsilon,n,m}
,
u_{1}
and
G:=\widecheck{u}_{1}-\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}
are bounded by a constant independent of
\varepsilon,m,n
and continuous due to Theorem
5.6
and the assumption of this theorem, there is a
\lambda_{0}>0
(depending only on
K
,
u_{1}
,
T
) such that

\displaystyle\sup_{(t,\mu)\in[0,T)\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}G(t,\mu)=\sup_{(t,\mu)\in[0,T)\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}\widecheck{u}_{1}(t,\mu)-\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\mu)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\leq\widecheck{u}_{1}(t_{0},\mu_{0})-\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t_{0},\mu_{0})+\lambda_{0}.

(6.11)

Recalling the definitions of
\rho_{1/\delta}
and
\varphi_{\delta}
in (
4.5
) and (
4.6
) for
\delta>0
respectively, we apply Theorem
4.8
to show that there exists
(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})\in[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
and a sequence
\left\{\left(t_{n},\mu_{n}\right)\right\}_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\subset[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
converging to
\left(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu}\right)
such that

\displaystyle\left[SW_{2}^{1/\delta}(\widetilde{\mu},\mu_{0})\right]^{2}\leq\rho_{1/\delta}\left((\widetilde{t},\widetilde{u}),(t_{0},\mu_{0})\right)\leq\lambda_{0}/\delta^{2}.

Therefore, by the stability result stated in
[
36
, Lemma 1]
, there is a constant
C_{d}>0
depending only on
d
such that

\displaystyle SW_{2}(\widetilde{\mu},\mu_{0})\leq\sqrt{2}SW_{2}^{1/\delta}(\widetilde{\mu},\mu_{0})+\dfrac{C_{d}}{\delta}\leq\dfrac{\sqrt{2\lambda_{0}}+C_{d}}{\delta}.

The values of
C_{d}
may change from line to line in this proof, but it still depends only on
d
. Furthermore, the first equality in (
4.7
) yields that
\kappa_{d}\mathcal{W}_{2}(\mu,\delta_{0})=SW_{2}(\mu,\delta_{0})
for all
\mu\in\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
, which implies

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{\mu},\mu_{0})

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{\mu},\delta_{0})+\mathcal{W}_{2}(\delta_{0},\mu_{0})

\displaystyle\leq C_{d}SW_{2}(\widetilde{\mu},\mu_{0})+C_{d}SW_{2}(\mu_{0},\delta_{0})+\mathcal{W}_{2}(\delta_{0},\mu_{0})

\displaystyle\leq C_{d}\dfrac{\sqrt{\lambda_{0}}+1}{\delta}+C_{d}SW_{2}(\mu_{0},\delta_{0})+\mathcal{W}_{2}(\delta_{0},\mu_{0})

\displaystyle\leq C_{d}\left(\dfrac{\sqrt{\lambda_{0}}+1}{\delta}+SW_{2}(\mu_{0},\delta_{0})\right).

(6.12)

Besides, item (2) in Theorem
4.8
implies that

\displaystyle G(t_{0},\mu_{0})=(\widecheck{u}_{1}-\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m})(t_{0},\mu_{0})=(u_{1}-v_{\varepsilon,n,m})(t_{0},\mu_{0})

\displaystyle\leq\widecheck{u}_{1}(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})-\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})-\delta^{2}\varphi_{\delta}(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})

\displaystyle\leq\widecheck{u}_{1}(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})-\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu}).

(6.13)

Step 1B. Proof of
\widetilde{t}<T
:

In this step, we shall prove that
\widetilde{t}<T
. Suppose not, we have
\widetilde{t}=T
and hence (
6.13
) implies that
u_{1}(t_{0},\mu_{0})-v_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t_{0},\mu_{0})\leq\widecheck{u}_{1}(T,\widetilde{\mu})-\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(T,\widetilde{\mu})
. Thus, for
\widehat{\mu}^{n,\overline{x}}:=\dfrac{1}{n}\displaystyle\sum^{n}_{j=1}\delta_{x^{j}}
, we can use equations (
6.9
), (
6.10
) to obtain that

\displaystyle u_{1}(t_{0},\mu_{0})-v_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t_{0},\mu_{0})\leq\,

\displaystyle\dfrac{e^{T-t_{0}}}{n}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\left[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\left(g(x^{i},\widetilde{\mu})-g^{i}_{n,m}(x^{1},\ldots,x^{n})\right)\bbotimes_{k=1}^{n}\widetilde{\mu}(dx^{k})\right]

\displaystyle=\,

\displaystyle\dfrac{e^{T-t_{0}}}{n}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\left[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\left(g(x^{i},\widetilde{\mu})-g(x^{i},\widehat{\mu}^{n,\overline{x}})\right)\bbotimes_{k=1}^{n}\widetilde{\mu}(dx^{k})\right]

\displaystyle+\dfrac{e^{T-t_{0}}}{n}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\left[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\left(g(x^{i},\widehat{\mu}^{n,\overline{x}})-g^{i}_{n,m}(x^{1},\ldots,x^{n})\right)\bbotimes_{k=1}^{n}\widetilde{\mu}(dx^{k})\right].

Using the Lipschitz property of
g
and (3) of Lemma
5.2
, we further have

\displaystyle u_{1}(t_{0},\mu_{0})-v_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t_{0},\mu_{0})\leq\,

\displaystyle Ke^{T-t_{0}}\left[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{W}_{1}(\widetilde{\mu},\widehat{\mu}^{n,\overline{x}})}\bbotimes_{k=1}^{n}\widetilde{\mu}(dx^{k})\right]

\displaystyle+\dfrac{2Ke^{T-t_{0}}}{n}\left[m^{dn}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\left(\sum^{n}_{i=1}|y^{i}|\right)\prod^{n}_{j=1}\Phi(my^{j})dy^{j}\right].

(6.14)

From
[
26
, Theorem 1]
, there is a constant
C_{d}>0
depending on
d
only and a sequence
\{h_{n}\}_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\subset\mathbb{R}
such that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\mathcal{W}_{1}(\widetilde{\mu},\widehat{\mu}^{n,\overline{x}})\bbotimes_{k=1}^{n}\widetilde{\mu}(dx^{k})\leq C_{d}\left[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|x|^{q_{0}}\widetilde{\mu}(dx)\right]^{1/q_{0}}h_{n}.

(6.15)

The sequence
h_{n}
and the number
q_{0}
are given by

h_{n}=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}n^{-1/2}+n^{-(q_{0}-1)/q_{0}}&\text{if }d=1;\\
n^{-1/2}\log(1+n)+n^{-(q_{0}-1)/q_{0}}&\text{if }d=2;\\
n^{-1/d}+n^{-(q_{0}-1)/q_{0}}&\text{if }d>2,\end{array}\right.\quad\text{with}\quad q_{0}=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}3/2&\text{if }d=1,2;\\
5/3&\text{if }d>2,\end{array}\right.

where
h_{n}
satisfies
\lim_{n\to\infty}h_{n}=0
. Together with (
6.12
), we further have

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{C_{d}h_{n}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\mathcal{W}_{1}(\widetilde{\mu},\widehat{\mu}^{n,\overline{x}})\bbotimes_{k=1}^{n}\widetilde{\mu}(dx^{k})\leq\left[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|x|^{2}\widetilde{\mu}(dx)\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{W}_{2}(\widetilde{\mu},\delta_{0})\leq C_{d}\left[\dfrac{\sqrt{\lambda_{0}}+1}{\delta}+SW_{2}(\mu_{0},\delta_{0})\right].

(6.16)

Hence, from (
6.14
), we have,

\displaystyle u_{1}(t_{0},\mu_{0})-v_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t_{0},\mu_{0})\leq\,

\displaystyle Ke^{T-t_{0}}C_{d}h_{n}\left[\dfrac{\sqrt{\lambda_{0}}+1}{\delta}+SW_{2}(\mu_{0},\delta_{0})\right]

\displaystyle+\dfrac{2Km^{dn}e^{T-t_{0}}}{n}\left[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\left(\sum^{n}_{i=1}|y^{i}|\right)\prod^{n}_{j=1}\Phi(my^{j})dy^{j}\right].

Passing
m\to\infty
and then
n\to\infty
subsequently, we use the fact that
h_{n}\to 0
to yield that

\displaystyle u_{1}(t_{0},\mu_{0})-\lim_{n\to\infty}\lim_{m\to\infty}v_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t_{0},\mu_{0})\leq 0.

Finally, using Lemmas
5.1
and
5.4
, we pass
\varepsilon\to 0
to conclude that
u_{1}(t_{0},\mu_{0})-v_{0}(t_{0},\mu_{0})\leq 0
which contradicts (
6.8
), and thus
\widetilde{t}<T
.

Step 1C. Estimate of
u_{1}-v_{\varepsilon,n,m}
:

Recalling from Theorems
4.8
,
5.6
, it implies that
\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}+\delta^{2}\varphi_{\delta}
is in
PC_{1}^{1,2}([0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}))
(see Definition
2.4
). From item (3) of Theorem
4.8
with
G:=\widecheck{u}_{1}-\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}
and (
6.11
), we observe that
\widecheck{u}_{1}-\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}-\delta^{2}\varphi_{\delta}
attains the maximum at
(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})
.
Suppose the maximum value is
M^{*}\in\mathbb{R}
, then
\widecheck{u}_{1}-\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}-\delta^{2}\varphi_{\delta}-M^{*}
attains the maximum with value zero at
(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})
.
We use the fact that
\widecheck{u}_{1}
is the viscosity subsolution of (
6.9
) to see that

\displaystyle 0\leq\,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}(\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}+\delta^{2}\varphi_{\delta})(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})-(\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}+\delta^{2}\varphi_{\delta})(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-M^{*}}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sup_{a\in A}\Bigg\{\widecheck{f}(\widetilde{t},x,\widetilde{\mu},a)+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big\{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}(\sigma\sigma^{\top})(\widetilde{t},x,a)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}(\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}+\delta^{2}\varphi_{\delta})(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})(x)\Big\}

\displaystyle\hskip 55.0pt+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}(\sigma^{0}\sigma^{0;\top})(\widetilde{t}\,)\mathcal{H}(\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}+\delta^{2}\varphi_{\delta})(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 55.0pt+\Big\langle b(\widetilde{t},x,\widetilde{\mu},a),\partial_{\mu}(\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}+\delta^{2}\varphi_{\delta})(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})(x)\Big\rangle\Bigg\}\widetilde{\mu}(dx).

Therefore, as
\widecheck{u}_{1}(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})-\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})-\delta^{2}\varphi_{\delta}(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-M^{*}}=0
and
\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}
solves (
6.10
), we further have

\displaystyle\hskip-10.0pt(\widecheck{u}_{1}-\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m})(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle\delta^{2}\partial_{t}\varphi_{\delta}(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sup_{a\in A}\Bigg\{\widecheck{f}(\widetilde{t},x,\widetilde{\mu},a)+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big\{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}(\sigma\sigma^{\top})(\widetilde{t},x,a)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}(\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}+\delta^{2}\varphi_{\delta})(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})(x)\Big\}

\displaystyle\hskip 105.0pt+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big\{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}(\sigma^{0}\sigma^{0;\top})(\widetilde{t}\,)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\mathcal{H}(\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}+\delta^{2}\varphi_{\delta})(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})\Big\}

\displaystyle\hskip 105.0pt+\Big\langle b(\widetilde{t},x,\widetilde{\mu},a),\partial_{\mu}(\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}+\delta^{2}\varphi_{\delta})(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})(x)\Big\rangle\Bigg\}\widetilde{\mu}(dx)

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\sup_{\overline{a}\in A^{n}}\Bigg\{\dfrac{1}{n}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\widecheck{f}^{i}_{n,m}(\widetilde{t},\overline{x},a^{i})+\dfrac{1}{2}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\textup{tr}\left[\Big((\sigma\sigma^{\top})(\widetilde{t},x^{i},a^{i})+(\sigma^{0}\sigma^{0;\top})(\widetilde{t}\,)+\varepsilon^{2}I_{d}\Big)\partial_{x^{i}x^{i}}^{2}\widecheck{\overline{v}}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(\widetilde{t},\overline{x})\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 60.0pt+\dfrac{1}{2}\sum^{n}_{i,j=1,i\neq j}\textup{tr}\left[(\sigma^{0}\sigma^{0;\top})(\widetilde{t}\,)\partial_{x^{i}x^{j}}^{2}\widecheck{\overline{v}}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(\widetilde{t},\overline{x})\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 60.0pt+\sum^{n}_{i=1}\Big\langle b^{i}_{n,m}(\widetilde{t},\overline{x},a^{i}),\partial_{x^{i}}\widecheck{\overline{v}}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(\widetilde{t},\overline{x})\Big\rangle\Bigg\}\bbotimes_{k=1}^{n}\widetilde{\mu}(dx^{k}).

(6.17)

We estimate term by term. First, Theorem
4.8
and
Assumption (A*)
tell us that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\varphi_{\delta}(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sup_{a\in A}\bigg\{\left\langle b(\widetilde{t},x,\widetilde{\mu},a),\partial_{\mu}\varphi_{\delta}(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})(x)\right\rangle+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big\{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}(\sigma\sigma^{\top})(\widetilde{t},x,a)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}\varphi_{\delta}(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})(x)\Big\}\bigg\}\widetilde{\mu}(dx)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big\{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}(\sigma^{0}\sigma^{0;\top})(\widetilde{t}\,)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\mathcal{H}\varphi_{\delta}(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq 4T+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}K\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{\mu}\varphi_{\delta}(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})(x)\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}}+\frac{K^{2}}{2}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}\varphi_{\delta}(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})(x)\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}}\widetilde{\mu}(dx)+\dfrac{K^{2}}{2}\left|\mathcal{H}\varphi_{\delta}(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})\right|

\displaystyle\leq 4T+K\sqrt{C_{d}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|x|^{2}\widetilde{\mu}(dx)+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|x|^{2}\mu_{0}(dx)+\dfrac{1+\lambda_{0}}{\delta^{2}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\hskip 10.0pt+\dfrac{K^{2}}{2}\sqrt{C_{d}}\delta\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|x|^{2}\mu_{0}(dx)+\dfrac{1+\lambda_{0}}{\delta^{2}}\right)^{1/2}+\frac{K^{2}}{2}C_{d}.

(6.18)

Second, we recall the representation of
\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\mu)=e^{t-t_{0}}v_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\mu)
in (
5.22
) and also (1), (2) of Theorem

. Inequality in (

\displaystyle\partial_{\mu}\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\mu)(x)=\sum^{n}_{i=1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d(n-1)}}\partial_{x^{i}}\widecheck{\overline{v}}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})\Big|_{x^{i}=x}\bbotimes^{n}_{k=1,k\neq i}\mu(dx^{k});

and (2) in Theorem

, Lemma

yield that

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\mu)=\sum^{n}_{i=1}\sum^{n}_{j=1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\partial_{x^{i}x^{j}}^{2}\widecheck{\overline{v}}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})\bbotimes^{n}_{k=1}\mu(dx^{k}).

Hence, we estimate the term

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sup_{a\in A}\bigg\{\widecheck{f}(\widetilde{t},y,\widetilde{\mu},a)+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big\{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}(\sigma\sigma^{\top})(\widetilde{t},y,a)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})(y)\Big\}

\displaystyle\hskip 40.0pt+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big\{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}(\sigma^{0}\sigma^{0;\top})(\widetilde{t}\,)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\mathcal{H}\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})\Big\}+\Big\langle b(\widetilde{t},y,\widetilde{\mu},a),\partial_{\mu}\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})(y)\Big\rangle\Bigg\}\widetilde{\mu}(dy)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sup_{a\in A}\Bigg\{\sum^{n}_{j=1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d(n-1)}}\dfrac{1}{n}\widecheck{f}(\widetilde{t},y,\widetilde{\mu},a)\bbotimes^{n}_{k=1,k\neq j}\widetilde{\mu}(dx^{k})

\displaystyle\hskip 80.0pt+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\bigg\{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}(\sigma\sigma^{\top})(\widetilde{t},y,a)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\sum^{n}_{j=1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d(n-1)}}\partial_{x^{j}x^{j}}^{2}\widecheck{\overline{v}}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})\Big|_{x^{j}=y}\bbotimes^{n}_{k=1,k\neq j}\widetilde{\mu}(dx^{k})\bigg\}

\displaystyle\hskip 80.0pt+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big\{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}(\sigma^{0}\sigma^{0;\top})(\widetilde{t}\,)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\sum^{n}_{j=1}\sum^{n}_{l=1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\partial_{x^{j}x^{l}}^{2}\widecheck{\overline{v}}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})\bbotimes^{n}_{k=1}\widetilde{\mu}(dx^{k})\Big\}

\displaystyle\hskip 80.0pt+\Big\langle b(\widetilde{t},y,\widetilde{\mu},a),\sum^{n}_{j=1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d(n-1)}}\partial_{x^{j}}\widecheck{\overline{v}}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})\Big|_{x^{j}=y}\bbotimes^{n}_{k=1,k\neq j}\widetilde{\mu}(dx^{k})\Big\rangle\Bigg\}\widetilde{\mu}(dy)

\displaystyle\leq\sum^{n}_{j=1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\sup_{a^{j}\in A}\Bigg\{\dfrac{1}{n}\widecheck{f}(\widetilde{t},x^{j},\widetilde{\mu},a^{j})+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\bigg\{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}(\sigma\sigma^{\top})(\widetilde{t},x^{j},a^{j})\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\partial_{x^{j}x^{j}}^{2}\widecheck{\overline{v}}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})\bigg\}

\displaystyle\hskip 80.0pt+\Big\langle b(\widetilde{t},x^{j},\widetilde{\mu},a^{j}),\partial_{x^{j}}\widecheck{\overline{v}}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})\Big\rangle\Bigg\}\bbotimes^{n}_{k=1}\widetilde{\mu}(dx^{k})

\displaystyle\hskip 10.0pt+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big\{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}(\sigma^{0}\sigma^{0;\top})(\widetilde{t}\,)\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\sum^{n}_{j=1}\sum^{n}_{l=1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\partial_{x^{j}x^{l}}^{2}\widecheck{\overline{v}}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(t,\overline{x})\bbotimes^{n}_{k=1}\widetilde{\mu}(dx^{k})\Big\}.

(6.19)

Putting (
6.18
) and (
6.19
) into (
6.17
), we use (
5.18
) and Theorem
4.8
to deduce that

\displaystyle\hskip-10.0pt(\widecheck{u}_{1}-\widecheck{v}_{\varepsilon,n,m})(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle 4T\delta^{2}+K\delta^{2}\sqrt{C_{d}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|x|^{2}\widetilde{\mu}(dx)+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|x|^{2}\mu_{0}(dx)+\dfrac{1+\lambda_{0}}{\delta^{2}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+\dfrac{K^{2}\delta^{2}}{2}\sqrt{C_{d}}\left(1+\lambda_{0}+\delta^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|x|^{2}\mu_{0}(dx)\right)^{1/2}+\frac{K^{2}\delta^{2}C_{d}}{2}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\sup_{a^{i}\in A}\Bigg\{\dfrac{1}{n}\widecheck{f}(\widetilde{t},x^{i},\widetilde{\mu},a^{i})-\dfrac{1}{n}\widecheck{f}^{i}_{n,m}(\widetilde{t},\overline{x},a^{i})-\dfrac{\varepsilon^{2}}{2}\textup{tr}\partial_{x^{i}x^{i}}^{2}\widecheck{\overline{v}}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(\widetilde{t},\overline{x})

\displaystyle\hskip 130.0pt+\left\langle b(\widetilde{t},x^{i},\widetilde{\mu},a^{i})-b^{i}_{n,m}(\widetilde{t},\overline{x},a^{i}),\partial_{x^{i}}\widecheck{\overline{v}}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(\widetilde{t},\overline{x})\right\rangle\Bigg\}\bbotimes^{n}_{k=1}\widetilde{\mu}(dx^{k})

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle 4T\delta^{2}+K\delta^{2}\sqrt{C_{d}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|x|^{2}\widetilde{\mu}(dx)+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|x|^{2}\mu_{0}(dx)+\dfrac{1+\lambda_{0}}{\delta^{2}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+\dfrac{K^{2}\delta^{2}}{2}\sqrt{C_{d}}\left(1+\lambda_{0}+\delta^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|x|^{2}\mu_{0}(dx)\right)^{1/2}+\frac{K^{2}\delta^{2}C_{d}}{2}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\sup_{a^{i}\in A}\Bigg\{\dfrac{e^{\widetilde{t}-t_{0}}}{n}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}f(\widetilde{t},x^{i},\widetilde{\mu},a^{i})-f^{i}_{n,m}(\widetilde{t},\overline{x},a^{i})\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}-\dfrac{\varepsilon^{2}}{2}\textup{tr}\partial_{x^{i}x^{i}}^{2}\widecheck{\overline{v}}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(\widetilde{t},\overline{x})

\displaystyle\hskip 130.0pt+\dfrac{C_{4}e^{\widetilde{t}-t_{0}}}{n}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}b(\widetilde{t},x^{i},\widetilde{\mu},a^{i})-b^{i}_{n,m}(\widetilde{t},\overline{x},a^{i})\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}\Bigg\}\bbotimes^{n}_{k=1}\widetilde{\mu}(dx^{k}).

(6.20)

We use
Assumption (A*)
and Lemma
5.2
to estimate the following term:

\displaystyle\hskip-10.0pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}f(\widetilde{t},x^{i},\widetilde{\mu},a^{i})-f^{i}_{n,m}(\widetilde{t},\overline{x},a^{i})\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}+C_{4}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}b(\widetilde{t},x^{i},\widetilde{\mu},a^{i})-b^{i}_{n,m}(\widetilde{t},\overline{x},a^{i})\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\big|$}}}}f(\widetilde{t},x^{i},\widetilde{\mu},a^{i})-f(\widetilde{t},x^{i},\widehat{\mu}^{n,\overline{x}},a^{i})\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}+\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}f(\widetilde{t},x^{i},\widehat{\mu}^{n,\overline{x}},a^{i})-f^{i}_{n,m}(\widetilde{t},\overline{x},a^{i})\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}

\displaystyle+C_{4}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}b(\widetilde{t},x^{i},\widetilde{\mu},a^{i})-b(\widetilde{t},x^{i},\widehat{\mu}^{n,\overline{x}},a^{i})\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}+C_{4}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}b(\widetilde{t},x^{i},\widehat{\mu}^{n,\overline{x}},a^{i})-b^{i}_{n,m}(\widetilde{t},\overline{x},a^{i})\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big|$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big|$}}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle K(1+C_{4}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{W}_{1}(\widetilde{\mu},\widehat{\mu}^{n,\overline{x}})}+K(1+C_{4})m\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\widetilde{t}-\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}T\wedge(\widetilde{t}-s)^{+}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\right|^{\beta}\phi(ms)ds

\displaystyle+K(1+C_{4})m^{dn}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\left(|y^{i}|+\dfrac{1}{n}\sum^{n}_{j=1}|y^{j}|\right)\prod^{n}_{k=1}\Phi(my^{k})dy^{k}.

(6.21)

Putting (
6.21
), (
6.13
) and (
6.16
) into (
6.20
), we see that

\displaystyle\hskip-10.0pt(u_{1}-v_{\varepsilon,n,m})(t_{0},\mu_{0})

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle 4T\delta^{2}+K\delta^{2}\sqrt{C_{d}}\left(\left[\dfrac{\sqrt{\lambda_{0}}+1}{\delta}+SW_{2}(\mu_{0},\delta_{0})\right]^{2}+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|x|^{2}\mu_{0}(dx)+\dfrac{1+\lambda_{0}}{\delta^{2}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+\dfrac{K^{2}\delta^{2}}{2}\sqrt{C_{d}}\left[1+\lambda_{0}+\delta^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|x|^{2}\mu_{0}(dx)\right]^{1/2}+\frac{K^{2}\delta^{2}C_{d}}{2}\noindent

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\dfrac{\varepsilon^{2}}{2}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\textup{tr}\partial_{x^{i}x^{i}}^{2}\widecheck{\overline{v}}_{\varepsilon,n,m}(\widetilde{t},\overline{x})\bbotimes^{n}_{k=1}\widetilde{\mu}(dx^{k})+e^{T-t_{0}}C_{d}h_{n}K(1+C_{4})\left[\dfrac{\sqrt{\lambda_{0}}+1}{\delta}+SW_{2}(\mu_{0},\delta_{0})\right]

\displaystyle+e^{T-t_{0}}K(1+C_{4})m\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\widetilde{t}-\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big[$}}}}}T\wedge(\widetilde{t}-s)^{+}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\displaystyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\textstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{285200}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{3.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\big]$}}}}}\right|^{\beta}\phi(ms)ds

\displaystyle+2e^{T-t_{0}}K(1+C_{4})\frac{m^{dn}}{n}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}\left(\sum^{n}_{j=1}|y^{j}|\right)\prod^{n}_{k=1}\Phi(my^{k})dy^{k}.

Using Lemma
5.3
,
5.5
and (
5.18
), we first take
\varepsilon\to 0^{+}
and then
m\to\infty
to obtain that

\displaystyle\hskip-10.0pt(u_{1}-\lim_{m\to\infty}v_{0,n,m})(t_{0},\mu_{0})

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle 4T\delta^{2}+K\delta^{2}\sqrt{C_{d}}\left(\left[\dfrac{\sqrt{\lambda_{0}}+1}{\delta}+SW_{2}(\mu_{0},\delta_{0})\right]^{2}+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|x|^{2}\mu_{0}(dx)+\dfrac{1+\lambda_{0}}{\delta^{2}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+\dfrac{K^{2}\delta^{2}}{2}\sqrt{C_{d}}\left[1+\lambda_{0}+\delta^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|x|^{2}\mu_{0}(dx)\right]^{1/2}+\frac{K^{2}\delta^{2}C_{d}}{2}

\displaystyle+e^{T-t_{0}}C_{d}h_{n}K(1+C_{4})\left[\dfrac{\sqrt{\lambda_{0}}+1}{\delta}+SW_{2}(\mu_{0},\delta_{0})\right].

By (
6.15
), (
6.16
), Lemmas
5.4
and
5.1
, we take
n\to\infty
and then
\delta\to 0^{+}
to obtain that

\displaystyle(u_{1}-v_{0})(t_{0},\mu_{0})=\left(u_{1}-\lim_{n\to\infty}\lim_{m\to\infty}v_{0,n,m}\right)(t_{0},\mu_{0})\leq 0,

which contradicts (
6.8
).

Part 2. Proof of
u_{2}\geq v_{0}
:

This step mainly follows the same pattern as in
Step II of
[
17
, Theorem 5.1]
as the presence of the common noise does not alter the arguments of the approximated control problems there. However, due to the technical gap presented in Remark
6.2
, we give a sketch of the new proof here. Following the arguments of the first part of Step II of the proof of
[
17
, Theorem 5.1]
, showing
u_{2}\geq v_{0}
is equivalent to showing

u_{2}(t,\mu)\geq v^{s}(t,\nu):=\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{t}^{s}f\left(r,X_{r}^{t,\xi,\mathfrak{a}},\mathbb{P}_{X_{r}^{t,\xi,\mathfrak{a}}}^{W^{0}},\mathfrak{a}\right)dr\right]+\mathbb{E}u_{2}\left(s,\mathbb{P}_{X_{s}^{t,\xi,\mathfrak{a}}}^{W^{0}}\right),

for every
(t,\mu)\in[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}),s\in(t,T],\xi\in L^{2}(\Omega,\mathcal{F}_{t},\mathbb{P};\mathbb{R}^{d})
, with
\mathcal{L}(\xi)=\mu
, and
\mathfrak{a}\in\mathcal{M}_{t}
, where
\mathcal{M}_{t}
denotes the set of
\mathcal{F}_{t}^{t}
-measurable random variables
\alpha:\Omega\to A
and
\nu:=\mathcal{L}(\xi,\mathfrak{a})
. Moreover, it is without loss of generality that
u_{2}(s,\cdot)
is Lipschitz continuous for every
s\in[0,T]
by the arguments of the first part of Step II of the proof of
[
17
, Theorem 5.1]
. Suppose that there exist
t_{0}\in[0,T)
,
s_{0}\in(t_{0},T]
and
\mu_{0}\in\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
and
\nu_{0}\in\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A)
, with
\mu_{0}
being the marginal of
\nu_{0}
on
\mathbb{R}^{d}
, such that

v^{s_{0}}(t_{0},\nu_{0})>u_{2}(t_{0},\mu_{0}).

As in the beginning of Part 1 of this proof, we can suppose that there exists some
q>2
such that
\nu_{0}\in\mathcal{P}_{q}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A)
.

The function
\widecheck{u}_{2}(t,\mu):=e^{t-t_{0}}u_{2}(t,\mu)
is a viscosity supersolution of the following equation

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}u(t,\mu)+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sup_{a\in A}\Bigg\{\widecheck{f}(t,x,\mu,a)+b(t,x,\mu,a)\cdot\partial_{\mu}u(t,\mu)(x)\\
&+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big(\sigma(t,x,a)\big[\sigma(t,x,a)\big]^{\top}\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}u(t,\mu)(x)\Big)\Bigg\}\mu(dx)+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big[\sigma^{0}(t)[\sigma^{0}(t)]^{\top}\mathcal{H}u(t,\mu)\Big]-u(t,\mu)\\
&=0\hskip 5.0pt\text{for $(t,\mu)\in[0,T)\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})$};\\
&u(T,\mu)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\widecheck{g}(x,\mu)\mu(dx)\hskip 10.0pt\text{for $\mu\in\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})$}.\end{aligned}\right.

where
\widecheck{f}(t,x,\mu,a):=e^{t-t_{0}}f(t,x,\mu,a)
and
\widecheck{g}(x,\mu):=e^{t-t_{0}}g(x,\mu)
. That is, for any
s_{0}\in[0,T]
, for any
\varphi\in PC_{1}^{1,2}([0,s_{0}]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A))
such that
\widecheck{u}_{2}-\varphi
attains a minimum with a value of
0
at
(t^{*},\nu^{*})\in[0,s_{0})\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A)
, then the following inequality holds:

\displaystyle 0\geq\,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\varphi(t^{*},\nu^{*})+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A}\Bigg\{\widecheck{f}(t^{*},x,\mu^{*},a)+b(t^{*},x,\mu^{*},a)\cdot\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t^{*},\nu^{*})(x,a)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big[\sigma(t^{*},x,a)\big[\sigma(t^{*},x,a)\big]^{\top}\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}\varphi(t^{*},\nu^{*})(x,a)\Big]\Bigg\}\nu^{*}(dx,da)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{2}\textup{tr}\Big[\sigma^{0}(t^{*})[\sigma^{0}(t^{*})]^{\top}\mathcal{H}_{d\times d}\varphi(t^{*},\nu^{*})\Big]-\varphi(t^{*},\nu^{*}),

(6.22)

where
\mu^{*}
is the marginal of
\nu^{*}
on
\mathbb{R}^{d}
. We regularize the coefficients for the control variable. Let
\Psi:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}^{+}
be a compactly supported smooth function satisfying
\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\Psi(y)dy=1
. We extend
b
and
f
trivially (for example,
f(t,x,\mu,a)=0
when
a\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
is not in
A
) on the space
[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})\times\mathbb{R}^{d}
, these extensions are still denoted by
b
and
f
. We further define the functions
\widetilde{b}^{i}_{n,m}
and
\widetilde{f}^{i}_{n,m}
by

\widetilde{b}^{i}_{n,m}(t,\overline{x},a)=m^{d}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}b^{i}_{n,m}(t,\overline{x},a-a^{\prime})\Psi(ma^{\prime})\,da^{\prime},

\widetilde{f}^{i}_{n,m}(t,\overline{x},a)=m^{d}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}f^{i}_{n,m}(t,\overline{x},a-a^{\prime})\Psi(ma^{\prime})\,da^{\prime},

for any
n,m\in\mathbb{N}
,
i=1,2,\ldots,n
,
\overline{x}=(x^{1},x^{2},\ldots,x^{n})\in\mathbb{R}^{dn}
and
(t,a)\in[0,T]\times A
. Recalling the compactly supported smooth function
\Phi
defined in Section
5.2
, we also define

u_{n,m}(t,\overline{x})=m^{nd}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}}u_{2}\left(t,\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\delta_{x^{j}-y^{j}}\right)\prod_{j=1}^{n}\Phi(my^{j})\,dy^{j},

and

\displaystyle v^{s_{0}}_{n,m}(t,\nu):=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[

\displaystyle\int_{t}^{s_{0}}\widetilde{f}^{i}_{n,m}\left(r,\overline{\widetilde{X}}^{1,m,t,\overline{\xi},\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0}}_{r},\ldots,\overline{\widetilde{X}}^{n,m,t,\overline{\xi},\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0}}_{r},\mathfrak{a}_{0}^{i}\right)dr

\displaystyle+u_{n,m}\left(s_{0},\overline{\widetilde{X}}^{1,m,t,\overline{\xi},\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0}}_{s_{0}},\ldots,\overline{\widetilde{X}}^{n,m,t,\overline{\xi},\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0}}_{s_{0}}\right)\bigg],

for any
t\in[0,s_{0}]
and
\nu\in\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A)
, where
\overline{\xi}=(\overline{\xi}^{1},\overline{\xi}^{2},\ldots,\overline{\xi}^{n})\in L^{2}(\Omega,\mathcal{F}_{t},\mathbb{P};\mathbb{R}^{dn})
,
\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0}=(\mathfrak{a}_{0}^{1},\mathfrak{a}_{0}^{2},\ldots,\mathfrak{a}_{0}^{n})\in(\mathcal{M}_{t})^{n}
such that
\mathcal{L}(\overline{\xi},\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0})=\nu\otimes\ldots\otimes\nu
and
\overline{\widetilde{X}}^{m,t,\overline{\xi},\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0}}_{s}=\Big(\overline{\widetilde{X}}^{1,m,t,\overline{\xi},\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0}}_{s},\ldots,\overline{\widetilde{X}}^{n,m,t,\overline{\xi},\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0}}_{s}\Big)
is the solution to (
5.4
) with
\overline{\alpha}=\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0}
,
\varepsilon=0
and
b
replacing by
\widetilde{b}^{i}_{n,m}
for
s\in[t,s_{0}]
.

For every
n,m\in\mathbb{N}
,
(t,\mu)\in[0,s_{0}]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
, we define
\widecheck{v}_{n,m}^{s_{0}}:=e^{t-t_{0}}v_{n,m}^{s_{0}}
and similarly for
\widecheck{\widetilde{f}^{i}}_{n,m}
and
\widecheck{u}_{n,m}
from
\widetilde{f}_{n,m}^{i}
and
u_{n,m}
. Let

\displaystyle\overline{v}_{n,m}^{s_{0}}(t,\overline{x},\overline{a}):=\widetilde{v}_{n,m}^{s_{0}}(t,\delta_{(x^{1},a^{1})}\otimes\ldots\otimes\delta_{(x^{n},a^{n})})

with
\overline{x}=\left(x^{1},\ldots,x^{n}\right)\in\mathbb{R}^{dn}
and
\overline{a}=\left(a^{1},\ldots,a^{n}\right)\in A^{n}
, where

\displaystyle\widetilde{v}_{n,m}^{s_{0}}(t,\overline{\nu}):=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{n}\sum^{n}_{i=1}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\int_{t}^{s_{0}}\widetilde{f}^{i}_{n,m}\left(s,\overline{\widetilde{X}}^{1,m,t,\overline{\xi},\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0}}_{s},\ldots,\overline{\widetilde{X}}^{n,m,t,\overline{\xi},\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0}}_{s},\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0}^{i}\right)ds

\displaystyle\hskip 110.0pt+u_{n,m}\left(\overline{\widetilde{X}}^{1,m,t,\overline{\xi},\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0}}_{s_{0}},\ldots,\overline{\widetilde{X}}^{n,m,t,\overline{\xi},\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0}}_{s_{0}}\right)\Bigg],

(6.23)

with
\overline{\nu}=\mathcal{L}(\overline{\xi},\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0})
. Moreover, by
[
17
, Theorem A.8]
, we deduce that
\widecheck{v}_{n,m}^{s_{0}}

can be represented by

\displaystyle\widecheck{v}_{n,m}^{s_{0}}(t,\nu)=e^{t-t_{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{dn}\times A^{n}}\overline{v}_{n,m}^{s_{0}}(t,\overline{x},\overline{a})\nu(dx^{1},da^{1})\ldots\nu(dx^{n},da^{n}),

and it could be shown by following the proofs of Lemma
5.5
, Theorem
5.6
and
[
17
, Theorem A.8]
that

1.

\overline{v}_{n,m}^{s_{0}}\in C^{1,2}\left(\left[0,s_{0}\right]\times\mathbb{R}^{dn}\times A^{n}\right)
and
v_{n,m}^{s_{0}}\in C^{1,2}\left(\left[0,s_{0}\right]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}\left(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A\right)\right)
;

2.

for any
i=1,\ldots,n
and
(t,\overline{x},\overline{a})\in\left[0,s_{0}\right]\times\mathbb{R}^{dn}\times A^{n}
, it holds that

\left|\partial_{x^{i}}\overline{v}_{n,m}^{s_{0}}(t,\overline{x},\overline{a})\right|\leq\frac{C_{K}}{n}

where the constant
C_{K}\geq 0
depends on
K
, but independent of
n,m
;

3.

if
t\in[0,s_{0}]
and
\nu\in\mathcal{P}_{q}\left(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A\right)
for some
q>2
,
then

\lim_{n\rightarrow+\infty}\lim_{m\rightarrow+\infty}v_{n,m}^{s_{0}}(t,\nu)=v^{s_{0}}(t,\nu);

4.

the function
\widecheck{v}_{n,m}^{s_{0}}(t,\nu)
solves the following equation classically:

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}u(t,\nu)+\overline{\mathbb{E}}\Bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\frac{1}{n}\widecheck{\widetilde{f}^{i}}_{n,m}(t,\overline{\xi},\mathfrak{a}_{0}^{i})+\Big\langle\widetilde{b}_{n,m}^{i}(t,\overline{\xi},\mathfrak{a}_{0}^{i}),\partial_{x^{i}}\overline{v}_{n,m}^{s_{0}}(t,\overline{\xi},\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0})\Big\rangle\\
&\hskip 90.0pt+\frac{1}{2}\text{tr}\left(\left[(\sigma\sigma^{\top})(t,\overline{\xi}^{i},\mathfrak{a}_{0}^{i})+(\sigma^{0}\sigma^{0;\top})(t)\right]\partial_{x^{i}x^{i}}^{2}\overline{v}_{n,m}^{s_{0}}(t,\overline{\xi},\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0})\right)\bigg\}\\
&\hskip 65.0pt+\dfrac{1}{2}\sum^{n}_{i,j=1,i\neq j}\textup{tr}\Big[(\sigma^{0}\sigma^{0;\top})(t)\partial_{x^{i}x^{j}}^{2}\overline{v}_{n,m}^{s_{0}}(t,\overline{\xi},\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0})\Big]\Bigg]\\
&-u(t,\nu)=0;\\
&u(s_{0},\nu)=\overline{\mathbb{E}}[\widecheck{u}_{n,m}(s_{0},\overline{\xi})],\end{aligned}\right.

for any
t\in[0,s_{0})
and
\nu\in\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A)
, where
\overline{\xi}=(\overline{\xi}^{1},\overline{\xi}^{2},\ldots,\overline{\xi}^{n})\in L^{2}(\Omega,\mathcal{F}_{t},\mathbb{P};\mathbb{R}^{dn})
,
\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0}=(\mathfrak{a}_{0}^{1},\mathfrak{a}_{0}^{2},\ldots,\mathfrak{a}_{0}^{n})\in(\mathcal{M}_{t})^{n}
such that
\mathcal{L}(\overline{\xi},\overline{\mathfrak{a}}_{0})=\nu\otimes\ldots\otimes\nu
.

Now, notice that
v_{n,m}^{s_{0}}
is bounded by a constant independent of
n,m
. As a consequence, there exists
\lambda\geq 0
, independent of
n,m
, satisfying

\displaystyle\sup_{[0,T]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A)}(\widecheck{v}_{n,m}^{s_{0}}-\widecheck{u}_{2})\leq(\widecheck{v}_{n,m}^{s_{0}}-\widecheck{u}_{2})(t_{0},\nu_{0})+\lambda.

(6.24)

Since
\widecheck{v}_{n,m}^{s_{0}}-\widecheck{u}_{2}
is bounded and continuous, by (
6.24
) and Theorem
4.8
applied on
[0,s_{0}]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A)
with
G=\widecheck{v}_{n,m}^{s_{0}}-\widecheck{u}_{2}
, we obtain that for every
\delta>0
there exist
\{(t_{k},\nu_{k})\}_{k\geq 1}\subset[0,s_{0}]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A)
converging to some
(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\nu})\in[0,s_{0}]\times\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times A)
and a function
\varphi_{\delta}
such that items (1)-(4) of Theorem
4.8
hold. It deduces that there is
M_{*}\in\mathbb{R}
such that
\widecheck{v}_{n,m}^{s_{0}}-\widecheck{u}_{2}-\delta^{2}\varphi_{\delta}-M_{*}
attains the maximum with a value of
0
at
(\widetilde{t},\widetilde{\mu})
. If
\widetilde{t}=s_{0}=T
, then we proceed as in Step 1B to get a contradiction. If
\widetilde{t}=s_{0}<T
, then it is straightforward to draw the contradiction by following (
6.13
) and using the definition of
\widetilde{v}_{n,m}^{s_{0}}(t,\overline{\nu})
in (
6.23
). If
\widetilde{t}<s_{0}
, we apply Definition
6.1
of supersolution and put
\varphi=-\widecheck{v}_{n,m}^{s_{0}}+\delta^{2}\varphi_{\delta}+M_{*}
in (
6.22
). Then we proceed as in Step 1C and utilize items (1)-(4) in the above to find a contradiction and conclude the proof.

∎
